# Extension: manual checks for the user

The user's pending visual and behaviour checks of the extension track
(`claude/docs/x64-port-scaling-dos-prompt.md`), grouped by the phase that makes each one possible.
Each item names what to look at and the D1 decision it checks (`docs/verify/ext-d1-decisions.md`;
DESIGN.md 12.5..12.11; FIDELITY F113..F348). Every item is **pending** unless its status says
**passed** (D4-4..D4-13, 2026-10-12; D4-15, D4-18, D4-19, D4-21..D4-24, 2026-10-15; D4-2, D4-14, D4-16, D4-17,
D4-20, D4-25..D4-29 and P47-2, 2026-10-15 about 19:40 UTC; D3-1..D3-10, D4a-1, D4-1 and D5-3, 2026-10-16 about
07:40..07:58 UTC; D5-1, D5-2, D5-4..D5-6, D5-14, D5-16, D5-18, D5-19 and D5-21..D5-23, 2026-10-16 about 09:45..10:06 UTC; D6c-1..D6c-7,

A
phase adds its result (passed, or the user's correction) when the user has checked it; D8 closes the
list.

How to run: `claude\port\build\x64\Release\civ64.exe` (RUNNING.md) with the DOS files found as
phase R describes (a `CivDos` folder or ZIP next to `civ64.exe`, or `--dos-data`). "Switch" means
Ctrl+Shift+Backspace or the window menu's Graphics > "Windows graphics" / "DOS graphics" (the "system menu" of older items is this window menu, opened by the icon at the left of the title bar or a right click on the title bar; since unit HM, build "hm", its zoom items are under Map zoom, its graphics items under Graphics, the 4:3 and scaling items under Display, and "Exit" is "Close").

| LP-8 | The `window` key in `civ64.cfg` is `window=left,top,right,bottom`: the window's outer rectangle in screen pixels (the primary screen's top-left is 0,0; `right` and `bottom` are one past the window, so the width is right - left). Move the window entirely off-screen (or remove a monitor it was on, or edit the value off-screen, for example `window=-4050,-5130,2137,1412`, with civ64 closed), close civ64 and start it again: the window comes back at the top-left corner of the primary screen's work area, with its size when that fits the screen, else the default size. A window left partly off-screen stays where it was while enough of its title bar to drag it (about two title-bar heights wide, at least half its height) is on a screen. ## FCE: Escape on Find City, the Civilopedia lists, production and city name (DOS graphics)

Found while checking MZS (2026-10-16): Escape does not close Find City (Ctrl+F) or the Civilopedia lists; record
`ext-FCE-record.md`. The original under Windows 3.1 ignores Escape there too (the game's dialogs have no Cancel
control Windows' Escape can reach, and the game's own Escape handling is never reached), so the Windows graphics stay
as they are. The DOS game's own screens cancel on Escape, so by the user's rule ("where DOS has a key for it (e.g.
Escape), that key does it") Escape now cancels Find City, the Civilopedia lists, the production list and the city
name box in the DOS graphics. Three-step cycle ALL_GREEN 2026-10-17 03:41:10 UTC after a fixed first run
(`build/fce-status.txt`; tested headless with real key presses). User build `claude/port/build/user-builds/fce/`, the
newest build (it contains everything in "tld", "d6e", "mzs", "si", "mzi", "ux5" and earlier; their items can be
checked there too). Load a game (File, Load; for example your MZTEST.SAV); switch the graphics with
Ctrl+Shift+Backspace or the system menu.
